## 28. Testing

Every system tests itself with the SDK's **test kit** (`@harness/sdk/testing`), in vitest, with no setup:

- **`testHub(contract, impl, { config, model })`** serves the system to a fake hub in memory; every `ctx.*` call it
  makes is recorded. `kit.call('<fn>', args)` takes the fn's own args type and gives `{ result, progress, hubCalls }`:
  the typed result, the progress it reported, and what it asked the hub for (`calls`, `forwards` to the model,
  `decisions`, `proposals`, `patches`, …). `kit.run` gives the outcome instead (for `invalid-input` or `failed`);
  `callFn(contract, impl, '<fn>', args)` serves, calls and stops in one line. Call `kit.stop()` after each test.
- **Models.** `mockModel([toolCall('search', { text: 'x' }), 'Saved.'])` answers `ctx.ai()` with scripted replies, in
  order; `mockModel({ recording: 'test/title.json' })` replays replies recorded once from a real model. A few tests may
  call a real free model with `realModel.it('…', async (model) => { … })` and pass `model` to `testHub`: they are
  marked `real` and skipped without `OPENROUTER_API_KEY`. The model gets the request as the hub's gateway sends it
  (the same tool schemas, strictness and token cap). A real model driving an agent (an authoring fn writing and
  checking a draft) needs a turn's time, not the test hub's 60 seconds: pass `liveTurn()` (four minutes) as the call's
  extra, `kit.call('make_api', { request }, liveTurn())`.
- **Kinds.** `kit.kindProps('<kind>', payload)` gives the problems the kind finds in a payload and the fn props it
  derives. `kit.runKind('<kind>', '<fn>', payload, args)` runs a fn of the kind as the hub runs a saved one (a code
  program, an API request) and gives its outcome, progress and hub calls.
- **Proposals.** A proposed run waits in `kit.hub.proposals` until the test settles it as its approver would:
  `kit.approve('<key, approval or run>', answer?)` runs it on the system (the answer at `args[ask.into]` when it asks
  one) and sends the `run` event `done`; `kit.decline(which, reason?)` sends it `cancelled` with the hub's words. A
  settled proposal can't be settled again, and outside a run its key then proposes afresh.
- **Instances people connect to.** `kit.hub.instanceConnect` is what the instance last said (`ctx.instanceConnect`);
  `kit.hub.instanceConnects` lists every time, including the one sent again on each reconnect.
- **Data.** The fake hub answers `ctx.data` from records in memory, like the hub (filters, sorts, text search, pages,
  versions, `expect`, person-owned types refused outside runs): `kit.records('<Type>')` reads what runs saved, and
  `kit.hub.data.save(...)` puts records in first. Another system's stored type goes in `kit.hub.storedTypes`.
- **Sensitive files, claims and mentions.** `kit.flags('<fn>', args)` gives the flags its `flags` hook answers, as
  the hub asks before a run. `kit.claims('<fn>', args)` gives what an agent's call would operate on: its
  `parallelClaims` filled from the args, joined with what its `claims` hook answers. `kit.mentions('<provider>', query)` gives a mention provider's options and the resource searches
  it made (answer them with `kit.hub.onMentionResources`).
- **Files and commands.** `scratchFolder({ git: true, files })` makes a temp folder (a git repository with the files
  committed) to run file and command fns in: change a file, add an untracked or ignored one, and check the change set
  or the flags a fn reports. `repo.dispose()` removes it.
- **Context.** `contextFolder(dir, contract)` checks the context folder: every file parses, every link resolves (fenced
  code is skipped), and `fns` maps each context to the fns it brings. Expect `problems` to be empty.
- **Components.** `preview({ name, module: new URL('./ResultView.vue', import.meta.url), states })` draws a component in
  named states (`empty`, `loading`, `error`, `typical`, `long`, or your own; plain JSON props). A normal run only
  bundles it; `harness test --previews` shoots each state at phone and desktop sizes in light and dark into
  `.harness-test/previews/gallery.html`, for a model or a person to review. `renderHtml(component, props)` renders
  in-process, for quick checks of what a state shows.

**`harness test [dir]`** runs a folder's tests from anywhere and prints the counts and each failure (`--json` gives the
whole report). `fs/install_system` runs them before an admin is asked, and the install card shows the result. Write
tests with every system: a call per fn, a scripted-model test per agent, a `contextFolder` check, and a preview of each
component.

Underneath, the kit's hub is **`FakeHub`** (`@harness/sdk/server/testing`): a hub in memory that
serves the real link spec, so the system under test runs its real link, handshake, runtime and wire checks. Only the
hub's own behaviour is scripted: child calls (`onCall`), handler answers (`onHandle`), LLM answers (`onLlm`), type
definitions (`onTypes`), resource reads (`onResource`), and setting values (`settings`).

- `new FakeHub({ config })` holds the system's config (absent: none yet, so a system with a config type waits as
  `needs-config`); `serve(contract, impl, { hub: 'ws://hub.test/link', state: 'unused' }, hub.host())` connects to it;
  `await running.ready()` waits until it is imported.
- `await hub.run('<system>/<fn>', args)` runs a fn and resolves with its outcome on the wire (`OutcomeWire`: `{ status:
  'done', result }`, `{ status: 'invalid-input', invalid }`, `{ status: 'failed', error }`, …).
- What the system sent is recorded: `hub.manifests`, `hub.attachments`, `hub.progress`, `hub.calls`, `hub.logs`, and
  the rest; `hub.peer()` calls the system's link endpoints directly (`embed`, `hook`, `mention`, …).
- `hub.configure(value)` changes the config; `hub.drop()` drops the link to test reconnects.

_From `scripts/docs/example/server/notes.test.ts`:_

```ts
const CONFIG = { defaultTags: ['Mine'] };

let kit: TestSystem<'notes', (typeof notes)['defs']> | undefined;
afterEach(async () => {
  await kit?.stop();
  kit = undefined;
});

/** Serve the notes system to a fake hub and wait until it is ready. */
async function start(): Promise<TestSystem<'notes', (typeof notes)['defs']>> {
  kit = await testHub(notes, createNotesImpl({ settings: notesEnv.parse() }), { config: CONFIG });
  return kit;
}

describe('notes over the link', () => {
  it('declares its stored type and its API groups', async () => {
    const manifest = await manifestOf(notes, { version: '0.1.0' });
    expect(manifest.types.find((type) => type.name === 'Note')?.records).toMatchObject({ shape: 'table', owner: 'shared' });
    expect(manifest.groups?.map((group) => group.name)).toEqual(['notes', 'sharing']);
    expect(manifest.fns.find((fn) => fn.name === 'review')?.group).toBe('sharing');
  });

  it('adds a note as a hub record with the default tags, and refuses a second with the same title', async () => {
    const notesKit = await start();
    const { result, hubCalls } = await notesKit.call('add', { title: 'Plan', text: 'Write the guide.', tags: ['Work'] });
    expect(result).toMatchObject({ title: 'Plan', tags: ['mine', 'work'] });
    expect(result.id).toMatch(/^rec_/);
    expect(hubCalls.attachments).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(notesKit.records('Note').map((record) => record.title)).toEqual(['Plan']);
    const again = await notesKit.run('add', { title: 'Plan', text: 'Again.' });
    expect(again.outcome).toMatchObject({ status: 'failed', error: { class: 'caller', code: 'taken' } });
  });

  it('finds by words and by tag in the hub, and answers nothing to search for as invalid input', async () => {
    const notesKit = await start();
    await notesKit.call('add', { title: 'Plan', text: 'Write the guide.', tags: ['work'] });
    await notesKit.call('add', { title: 'Milk', text: 'Buy oat milk.', tags: ['home'] });
    expect((await notesKit.call('find', { query: 'oat' })).result.notes.map((note) => note.title)).toEqual(['Milk']);
    expect((await notesKit.call('find', { tag: 'work' })).result.notes.map((note) => note.title)).toEqual(['Plan']);
    const outcome = await notesKit.run('find', {});
    expect(outcome.outcome).toMatchObject({ status: 'invalid-input', invalid: { problems: [{ path: 'query' }] } });
  });

  it('tidies tags a page at a time, saving only the notes that change', async () => {
    const notesKit = await start();
    await notesKit.call('add', { title: 'Plan', text: 'x', tags: [] });
    const id = notesKit.records('Note')[0]?.id;
    if (id === undefined) { throw new Error('add saved no record'); }
    // a tag written another way, as another system might have saved it
    notesKit.hub.data.save({ type: 'notes/Note', id, value: { title: 'Plan', text: 'x', tags: ['Work ', 'work'], pinned: false } });
    expect((await notesKit.call('tidy', {})).result).toEqual({ merged: 1 });
    expect(notesKit.records('Note')[0]?.value).toMatchObject({ tags: ['work'] });
  });
});
```

Also unit-test the parts behind each interface (a parser, a client of an outside service) directly, with temp
folders and fixed clocks. Keep
tests that call real services (a model, an API) in `*.live.test.ts` files, marked and minimal; `npm run test:live` runs
them, `npm test` skips them.

**Compile-time tests.** For a mistake the types must refuse, write it under `// @ts-expect-error <why>`: if it ever
compiles, `npm run typecheck` fails. `expectType<Equal<A, B>>(true)` asserts two types are the same. The SDK's own
compile-time tests are the source of **Common mistakes**.

**Before you finish:** `npm run typecheck` (every project, the new system included once it is in the root
`tsconfig.json` references), `npx vitest run systems/<name>`, and `npm run lint`.

A context you build by hand (for a unit test of a lifecycle hook or a fn without the fake hub) needs `ctx.propose`
and `ctx.decide`: spread `NO_ASKS` beside `TEST_PLACE`. Proposing then fails and the decision model answers `off`;
`FakeHub` records proposals (`hub.proposals`), answers `decide` with `hub.onDecide`, and asks a system for a preview
with `hub.preview(...)`.

### Reference: testing

#### `FakeHub`

class · `import { FakeHub } from '@harness/sdk/server/testing'`

A hub in memory for testing systems.

```ts
class FakeHub {
  readonly systems: Peers<SystemLinkSpec>;
  readonly app: Server<HubLinkSpec>;
  readonly process: FakeProcess;
  /** Every hello it was sent, as sent. */
  readonly hellos: Hello[];
  readonly manifests: Manifest[];
  readonly patches: ManifestPatch[];
  readonly calls: Array<HubBody<'call'>>;
  readonly handled: Array<HubBody<'handle'>>;
  /** The names each `types` request asked for. */
  readonly typeRequests: Array<HubBody<'types'>['names']>;
  readonly controls: RunControl[];
  readonly progress: Array<HubBody<'progress'>>;
  readonly allowed: Array<HubBody<'allow'>>;
  readonly undos: Array<HubBody<'undoWith'>>;
  readonly attachments: Array<HubBody<'attach'>>;
  readonly logs: LogLine[];
  /** Refreshed (or expired) credentials systems handed back (`connectionUpdate`). */
  readonly connectionUpdates: Array<HubBody<'connectionUpdate'>>;
  /** How the instance said people connect to it (`instanceConnect`), in order; it is said again on every welcome. */
  readonly instanceConnects: Array<HubBody<'instanceConnect'>>;
  /** Fns a run handed to its person (`shareFn`). */
  readonly shares: Array<HubBody<'shareFn'>>;
  /** Whether a run's person may use a fn of its system (`usableFns`); every fn by default. */
  usable: (run: HubBody<'usableFns'>['run'], fn: string) => boolean;
  readonly changes: Array<HubBody<'resourcesChanged'>['changes'][number]>;
  readonly forwards: LlmForward[];
  /** Every non-chat model call forwarded (`model`). */
  readonly models: ModelForward[];
  /** What waiting systems said for the pairing page (`pairingInfo`). */
  readonly pairingInfos: PairingInfo[];
  /** What the system told the admins (`ctx.notifyAdmins`), in order. */
  readonly notices: AdminNotice[];
  /** What a host's supervisor refused for the systems it hosts (`reportRefusal`), in order. */
  readonly hostRefusals: HubBody<'reportRefusal'>[];
  /**
  * Runs systems proposed (`propose`), with the run and approval the fake hub made; one per open `key` (outside a run a
  * settled key proposes afresh, as on the real hub). Settle one with `approveProposal` or `declineProposal`.
  */
  readonly proposals: Array<HubBody<'propose'> & ProposedRun>;
  /** What systems asked the decision model (`decide`). */
  readonly decisions: Array<HubBody<'decide'>>;
  /** Example changes systems sent (`examples`). */
  readonly exampleChanges: Array<HubBody<'examples'>>;
  /** Pairing tokens approved install runs asked for (`grantPairing`), with the grant the fake hub made. */
  readonly pairingGrants: Array<PairingGrantRequest & PairingGrant>;
  /** Paired keys by system name. */
  readonly keys: Map<string, string>;
  /** Session state by owner (`run:<id>` / `delivery:<id>`). */
  readonly state: Map<string, { values: JsonObject; version: number; }>;
  /** Effective setting values by qualified name. */
  readonly settings: Map<string, z.core.util.JSONType>;
  /** Content by hash. */
  readonly content: Map<string, Uint8Array<ArrayBufferLike>>;
  /** A resource's outline by URI (`resourceSections`); a URI not here has no sections. */
  readonly sections: Map<string, { key: SectionKey; path: string[]; title: string; start: number; end: number; embedText?: string | undefined; form?: "code" | "description" | "table" | "text" | "markdown" | "pages" | "ocr" | "transcript" | "thumbnail" | undefined; }[]>;
  /** The hub's kept fns (`hub/fns_save`, `hub/fns_get`, `searchFns`, `fnExamples`), in memory. */
  readonly fns: FakeHubFns;
  /**
  * The data system in memory (`ctx.data`): records of the stored types the system declared, or of another system's
  * types put in `storedTypes`, answered like the hub does, as `TEST_USER`. Read them back with `data.of(type)`.
  */
  readonly data: FakeRecords;
  /** Other systems' stored types the data system knows, by qualified name. */
  readonly storedTypes: Map<`${string}/${string}`, StoredTypeDecl>;
  /** Resource searches a mention provider made (`ctx.resources.search` in `search`), in order. */
  readonly mentionSearches: MentionResourceSearch[];
  /** Answers a mention provider's resource search (default: no hits). */
  onMentionResources: (search: MentionResourceSearch) => Promise<ResourceHit[]>;
  /** The hub's widget fns (`hub/read_table`, `hub/visuals`, `hub/row_actions`), in memory. */
  readonly widgets: FakeHubWidgets;
  /**
  * Settles a child call. Default: `hub/fns_save` and `hub/fns_get` by `fns`, the widget fns by `widgets`, anything else
  * done with `null`.
  */
  onCall: (call: FakeHub['calls'][number]) => Promise<RunSettled>;
  /** Answers a run's handler. Default: refused. */
  onHandle: (call: FakeHub['handled'][number]) => Promise<HandlerAnswer>;
  /** Answers `ctx.types.definitions` (the real hub sends the closure, dependencies first). Default: every name missing. */
  onTypes: (names: HubBody<'types'>['names']) => Promise<TypeDefinitions>;
  /** Answers a kind check's look-up of another system's fn (link `fnShape`). Default: not known. */
  onFnShape: (name: HubBody<'fnShape'>['name']) => Promise<{ fn?: KindFnShape }>;
  /** Streams an LLM answer. Default: the text `ok`. */
  onLlm: (forward: LlmForward) => AsyncIterable<LlmForwardChunk>;
  /** Reads a resource for `ctx.resources` (the real hub routes to the system that serves it). Default: not found. */
  onResource: (read: HubBody<'resource'>) => Promise<ResourceRead>;
  /**
  * Answers a non-chat model call: embeddings by text length, images and speech as stored content, a fixed
  * transcript. Each answer has a fresh `ModelCall` id and a small cost.
  */
  onModel: (forward: ModelForward) => Promise<ModelForwardResult>;
  /** Answers `ctx.decide`. Default: `off` (no decision model), so the system goes by its scores. */
  onDecide: (request: HubBody<'decide'>) => Promise<DecideAnswer>;
  /** Answers `models` (`ctx.ai()` lists them). Default: none, so calls go to `hub/auto`. */
  onModels: () => Promise<HubModelInfo[]>;
  /** Answers `ctx.fleet`. Default: no instances listed. */
  onFleet: () => Promise<FleetInstance[]>;
  /** Controls a child run. Default: accepted. */
  onControl: (control: RunControl) => Promise<{ accepted: boolean; reason?: string }>;
  constructor(options: FakeHubOptions = {});
  /** The transport a system under test connects with. */
  transport(): LinkTransport;
  /** A `LinkHost` wired to this hub, with in-memory keys and journal, no signals and a fake process. */
  host(deps?: Partial<LinkHostDeps>): LinkHost;
  /** The connected system's peer (its `systemLinkSpec`, to call directly). */
  peer(): Peer<SystemLinkSpec>;
  /** Whether a system is connected and welcomed. */
  get connected(): boolean;
  /** Wait until a system is connected and welcomed. */
  whenConnected(timeout?: number): Promise<Peer<SystemLinkSpec>>;
  /** Drop the link (both sides see it close); it reconnects after `after` ms. */
  drop(after?: number): void;
  /** Approve the pending pairing of a system: its key is remembered and it is told to say hello again. */
  approve(name: string): Promise<void>;
  /** Set (or change) the system's config and tell it. Absent fields are dropped, as JSON would. */
  configure(value: unknown): Promise<void>;
  /** The current config version (0 when none is set). */
  get configVersion(): number;
  /** A `RunInfo` for a run of `fn`. */
  runInfo(fn: QualifiedName, overrides?: Partial<RunInfo>): RunInfo;
  /** Build a run request for `fn` (`system/local`). */
  request(fn: QualifiedName, args: Json, extra?: Partial<Omit<RunRequest, "fn" | "args">>): RunRequest;
  /** Ask the connected system to get ready for a data export; resolves with where its data folder is. */
  exportData(timeout?: number): Promise<{ data: string; }>;
  /** Begin connecting a person on a per-person system (`connectStart`): where to send them. */
  connectStart(body: SystemBody<"connectStart">): Promise<{ url: string; }>;
  /** Finish connecting a person (`connectFinish`) with what the redirect or form brought. */
  connectFinish(body: SystemBody<"connectFinish">): Promise<ConnectResult>;
  /** A person disconnected (`disconnect`). */
  disconnect(body: SystemBody<"disconnect">): Promise<void>;
  /** Ask the connected system what a run would do (`preview`), as the hub does before an approval. */
  preview(body: SystemBody<"preview">): Promise<PreviewAnswer>;
  /** Ask the connected system which sensitive files a run would touch (`flags`), as the hub does before a run. */
  flags(body: SystemBody<"flags">): Promise<FlagsAnswer>;
  /** Ask the connected system what an agent's call would operate on (`claims`), as the hub does before the call starts. */
  claims(body: SystemBody<"claims">): Promise<ClaimsAnswer>;
  /**
  * Ask the connected system for `@` options (`mentions`) as `TEST_USER`, with a fresh mention request, so its provider
  * may search resources (`ctx.resources.search`, answered by `onMentionResources` and kept in `mentionSearches`).
  */
  mentions(provider: string, query: string): Promise<WireMentionOption[]>;
  /** Ask the connected system what a fn of one of its kinds costs (`estimate`), as the hub does for `FnFacts.cost`. */
  estimate(body: SystemBody<"estimate">): Promise<KindEstimateAnswer>;
  /** Run a fn on the connected system and wait for its outcome. */
  run(fn: QualifiedName, args: Json, extra?: Partial<Omit<RunRequest, "fn" | "args">>): Promise<OutcomeWire>;
  /** How the instance last said people connect to it (`instanceConnect`); `undefined` when it never said. */
  get instanceConnect(): { share: boolean; title: string; flow?: { flow: "oauth"; } | { flow: "form"; form: TypeDef<any>; } | undefined; } | undefined;
  /** An open proposal by its key, approval id or run id. */
  openProposal(which: string): HubBody<"propose"> & ProposedRun;
  /**
  * Approve a proposed run (by its key, approval id or run id) as its approver would: `answer` (required when the
  * proposal has an `ask`) is set at `args[ask.into]`, the run goes ahead on the system with `approved`, and the
  * system hears it settle through the `run` event (`done`, or how it ended). Resolves with the run's outcome.
  */
  approveProposal(which: string, options?: { readonly answer?: Json; readonly by?: ActorId; }): Promise<OutcomeWire>;
  /**
  * Decline a proposed run as its approver would: it settles `cancelled`, and the system hears it through the `run`
  * event, with the hub's words for it ("Declined by …: reason") as the status text.
  */
  declineProposal(which: string, reason?: string): Promise<void>;
  /** Store content (as an upload would) and return its ref. */
  put(bytes: Uint8Array, mime: string): ContentRef;
}
```

#### `FakeHubOptions`

interface · `import { FakeHubOptions } from '@harness/sdk/server/testing'`

How the fake hub behaves.

```ts
interface FakeHubOptions {
  /**
   * The config it holds for the system, as a plain value (absent fields are dropped, as JSON would). Absent: none yet
   * (the system waits as `needs-config`, unless it needs none).
   */
  config?: unknown;
  /** Ask for pairing the first time a name says hello (default false: every key is accepted). */
  pairing?: boolean;
  /** How long a pairing request lasts, in ms (default ten minutes). */
  pairingTtl?: number;
  /** The welcome's start info. */
  start?: { first: boolean; upgradedFrom?: string };
  maxChunk?: number;
  /** The instance name the welcome gives (an admin renamed it at approval); default: the one the system asked for. */
  instance?: InstanceName;
  /** An auto-pair grant every welcome carries. */
  autoPair?: AutoPairGrant;
  /** Refuse every hello that brings an auto-pair secret, as the hub does for an expired or revoked one. */
  refuseAutoPair?: boolean;
}
```

Refers to: [`AutoPairGrant`](#core-autopairgrant), [`InstanceName`](#core-instancename).

#### `NO_ASKS`

const · `import { NO_ASKS } from '@harness/sdk/server/testing'`

`ctx.propose`, `ctx.decide` and `ctx.ai` for a hand-made test context that has no hub: proposing fails (the test
didn't expect it), the decision model is off, and `ctx.ai()` is an aeye `AI` over `FakeModelLink` answering `ok`.
Spread it in beside `TEST_PLACE`; `FakeHub` serves the real ones.

```ts
const NO_ASKS: Proposals & Decisions & Fleets & FnSearch & AiSource & AdminNotices & InstanceConnects;
```

type · `import { HubBody } from '@harness/sdk/server/testing'`

type · `import { SystemBody } from '@harness/sdk/server/testing'`

#### `TestSystem`

class · `import { TestSystem } from '@harness/sdk/testing'`

A system served to a fake hub: run its fns and kinds, read what it asked the hub for, and stop it.

```ts
class TestSystem<N extends string, C extends ContractShape> {
  /** The fake hub, to script (`onCall`, `onDecide`, …) and read everything it recorded. */
  readonly hub: FakeHub;
  readonly running: RunningSystem<N>;
  readonly contract: SystemContract<N, C>;
  constructor(contract: SystemContract<N, C>, hub: FakeHub, running: RunningSystem<N>);
  /** Run a fn with typed args; its outcome (a done result decoded to its type), progress and hub calls. */
  run<K extends FnNameOf<C>>(name: K, args: FnArgsOf<C, K>, extra?: Partial<Omit<RunRequest, "fn" | "args">>): Promise<TestRun<FnResultOf<C, K>>>;
  /** Run a fn that should finish: its typed result, progress and hub calls; `CallNotDone` when it doesn't. */
  call<K extends FnNameOf<C>>(name: K, args: FnArgsOf<C, K>, extra?: Partial<Omit<RunRequest, "fn" | "args">>): Promise<TestCall<FnResultOf<C, K>>>;
  /**
  * Run a fn of one of the contract's kinds, as the hub runs a saved fn of the kind (a code program, an API request):
  * `fn` is its local name, `payload` the kind's typed payload, `args` the fn's args as JSON. Its outcome (the result
  * as JSON, since a kind fn's result type is its own), progress and hub calls.
  */
  runKind<K extends KindNameOf<C>>(kind: K, fn: string, payload: KindPayloadOf<C, K>, args: Json, extra?: Partial<Omit<RunRequest, "fn" | "args" | "kind" | "payload">>): Promise<TestRun<Json>>;
  /** Approve a run the system proposed (by its key, approval id or run id), with the answer its `ask` wants; see `FakeHub.approveProposal`. */
  approve(proposal: string, answer?: Json): Promise<OutcomeWire>;
  /** Decline a run the system proposed, with the approver's reason; see `FakeHub.declineProposal`. */
  decline(proposal: string, reason?: string): Promise<void>;
  /** What a kind makes of a payload, as the hub asks it when a fn of the kind is saved. */
  kindProps<K extends KindNameOf<C>>(kind: K, payload: KindPayloadOf<C, K>): Promise<KindProps>;
  /**
  * The sensitive files a run of a fn with these args would touch, as the hub asks before running it (`flags`): the fn's
  * own `flags(args, ctx)`; none for a fn that declares no `flags`.
  */
  flags<K extends FnNameOf<C>>(name: K, args: FnArgsOf<C, K>): Promise<SensitivityFlag[]>;
  /**
  * What an agent's call of a fn with these args would operate on, as the hub works it out before starting it: its
  * `parallelClaims` filled from the args, then (for a fn with `dynamicClaims`) what its own `claims(args, ctx)` adds.
  */
  claims<K extends FnNameOf<C>>(name: K, args: FnArgsOf<C, K>): Promise<string[]>;
  /** A mention provider's options for what the person typed after `@`, with any resource searches it made. */
  mentions(provider: keyof C["mentions"] & string, query: string): Promise<{ readonly options: WireMentionOption[]; readonly searches: FakeHub["mentionSearches"]; }>;
  /** The records of one of the system's stored types the run left in the fake hub, newest change first. */
  records(type: keyof C["types"] & string): DataRecord[];
  /**
  * The visuals that would draw a table, best first, as the hub, the widget agent and the web app rank them: the
  * built-in families and this system's own `HUB_VISUALS` contributions (`rankedVisuals` over `resultFacts`). Check that
  * a visual you declare is offered for the data it is for, and with the binding you expect.
  */
  visuals(table: ResultTable): VisualRank[];
  /** Stop the system. */
  stop(): Promise<void>;
}
```

Refers to: [`DataRecord`](#core-datarecord), [`Json`](#core-json), [`OutcomeWire`](#core-outcomewire), [`ResultTable`](#core-resulttable), [`RunRequest`](#core-runrequest), [`SensitivityFlag`](#core-sensitivityflag), [`VisualRank`](#core-visualrank).

#### `HubCalls`

interface · `import { HubCalls } from '@harness/sdk/testing'`

What a run asked of the hub, by endpoint, in order: the records the fake hub made while it ran.

```ts
interface HubCalls {
  /** Child fn calls (`ctx.call`, deps, `hub/*` fns). */
  readonly calls: FakeHub['calls'];
  /** Chat model requests (`ctx.ai()`, `ctx.llm`). */
  readonly forwards: FakeHub['forwards'];
  /** Other model calls: embeddings, images, speech. */
  readonly models: FakeHub['models'];
  /** Questions for the decision model (`ctx.decide`). */
  readonly decisions: FakeHub['decisions'];
  /** Runs it proposed (`ctx.propose`). */
  readonly proposals: FakeHub['proposals'];
  /** Handlers it asked its caller to answer. */
  readonly handled: FakeHub['handled'];
  readonly attachments: FakeHub['attachments'];
  readonly shares: FakeHub['shares'];
  /** Type definitions it asked for (`ctx.types.definitions`). */
  readonly typeRequests: FakeHub['typeRequests'];
  /** Example changes it sent. */
  readonly examples: FakeHub['exampleChanges'];
  /** Runtime contract changes (fns, context, … added or removed). */
  readonly patches: FakeHub['patches'];
}
```

#### `mockModel`

function · `import { mockModel } from '@harness/sdk/testing'`

A model for fast unit tests. Given steps, it answers each chat forward with the next step (then `done` once they
run out); given a recording, it replays it, recording first from a real model when the file is missing.

```ts
const model = mockModel([toolCall('search', { text: 'notes' }), 'Saved notes/by_tag.']);
const kit = await testHub(notes, impl, { model });
```

````ts
/** A model for fast unit tests. Given steps, it answers each chat forward with the next step (then `done` once they
run out); given a recording, it replays it, recording first from a real model when the file is missing.

```ts
const model = mockModel([toolCall('search', { text: 'notes' }), 'Saved notes/by_tag.']);
const kit = await testHub(notes, impl, { model });
``` */
function mockModel(steps: readonly ScriptStep[], options?: { models?: readonly HubModelInfo[]; }): FakeModelLink;
function mockModel(recording: RecordingOptions): RecordedModelLink;
````

Refers to: [`HubModelInfo`](#core-hubmodelinfo).

type · `import { ScriptStep } from '@harness/sdk/testing'`

#### `ModelRecording`

const, type · `import { ModelRecording } from '@harness/sdk/testing'`

A recording of a real model's replies, one per chat forward, in order.

```ts
type ModelRecording = {
  replies: ({
    type: "chunk";
    chunk: {
      content?: string | undefined;
      reasoning?: string | undefined;
      toolCallNamed?: {
        id: string;
        name: string;
        arguments: string;
      } | undefined;
      toolCallArguments?: {
        id: string;
        name: string;
        arguments: string;
      } | undefined;
      toolCallMore?: {
        id: string;
        name: string;
        arguments: string;
      } | undefined;
      toolCall?: {
        id: string;
        name: string;
        arguments: string;
      } | undefined;
      finishReason?: "refusal" | "length" | "stop" | "tool_calls" | "content_filter" | undefined;
      refusal?: string | undefined;
      usage?: {
        input: number;
        output: number;
        cached?: number | undefined;
        reasoning?: number | undefined;
      } | undefined;
    };
  } | {
    type: "descriptors";
    tools: Record<string, string>;
    output?: string | undefined;
  } | {
    type: "done";
    usage: {
      input: number;
      output: number;
      cached?: number | undefined;
      reasoning?: number | undefined;
    };
    cost: number;
    interaction: string;
    model: string;
  } | {
    type: "error";
    message: string;
    kind: "provider" | "limit" | "model" | "tool";
  })[][];
};
```

Refers to: [`LlmForwardChunk`](#core-llmforwardchunk).

#### `liveTurn`

function · `import { liveTurn } from '@harness/sdk/testing'`

A run's time for a call that drives a real model through an agent (authoring a fn): four minutes, like a turn on the
hub (`HARNESS_TURN_TIME`, ten minutes by default), not the test hub's 60-second default, which a real model writing
and checking a program can pass without anything being wrong. Pass it as `kit.call`'s extra.

```ts

```

class · `import { FakeRecords } from '@harness/sdk/testing'`

class FakeRecords {

## 29. Common mistakes

### Mistakes the compiler refuses

Each of these is a compile-time test in `packages/sdk/typetests`: the code under the comment does not compile, for the
reason given. If you see one of these errors, the fix is in the reason.

- **An example's args must fit the fn's args type.**

  ```ts
  fn({ description: 'x', args: g.obj({ query: g.text() }), returns: g.text(), examples: [{ args: { query: 3 } }] });
  ```
- **An example must give the required args.**

  ```ts
  fn({ description: 'x', args: g.obj({ query: g.text() }), returns: g.text(), examples: [{ args: {} }] });
  ```
- **A context link's relation is one a system may declare (`learned` is the hub's to find).**

  ```ts
  context({ description: 'x', content: 'y', links: [{ to: notes.fns.find, relation: 'learned' }] });
  ```
- **A context link points at a ref or a resource, not a bare name.**

  ```ts
  context({ description: 'x', content: 'y', links: [{ to: 'notes/find', relation: 'brings' }] });
  ```
- **A parent is a context ref.**

  ```ts
  context({ description: 'x', content: 'y', parent: notes.fns.find });
  ```
- **A detail is listed, signature or full.**

  ```ts
  ctx.expose(notes.fns.open, 'everything');
  ```
- **Only fn refs can be exposed.**

  ```ts
  ctx.expose(notes.types.Note);
  ```
- **Problems are a message or { path, message } items.**

  ```ts
  void ctx.invalid([{ field: 'query' }]);
  ```
- **A speech format the hub speaks.**

  ```ts
  void ctx.llm.speak({ text: 'hi', format: 'flac' });
  ```
- **An image needs a prompt.**

  ```ts
  void ctx.llm.image({ size: '1x1' });
  ```
- **The run is the runtime's to fill in.**

  ```ts
  void ctx.llm.image({ prompt: 'x', run: 'run_x' });
  ```
- **A viewer either replaces the preview or adds a tab.**

  ```ts
  client(notes, { resources: { markdown: { component: MarkdownTab, mode: 'popup' } } });
  ```
- **A viewer's props are a ResourceView.**

  ```ts
  client(notes, { resources: { markdown: { component: WrongViewer, mode: 'tab' } } });
  ```
- **Forms are core's form kinds.**

  ```ts
  void view.text('rendered');
  ```
- **A mode is one of core's chat modes.**

  ```ts
  agent({ description: 'x', args: g.obj({}), returns: g.text(), instructions: 'x', mode: { type: 'loose' } });
  ```
- **A static protocol is auto, native or text.**

  ```ts
  agent({ description: 'x', args: g.obj({}), returns: g.text(), instructions: 'x', mode: { type: 'static', protocol: 'xml' } });
  ```
- **There is no such hub fn.**

  ```ts
  void hub.fns.find_everything;
  ```
- **A resource read needs its uri.**

  ```ts
  void ((args: Infer<typeof hub.fns.resource_read.args>) => args)({});
  ```
- **`paht` is not one of the args.**

  ```ts
  fn({ description: 'Write a file.', args, returns: g.bool(), parallelClaims: ['file:{args.paht}'] });
  ```
- **A claim reads only the args.**

  ```ts
  fn({ description: 'Write a file.', args, returns: g.bool(), parallelClaims: ['file:{output.path}'] });
  ```
- **Claims are strings.**

  ```ts
  claims: (args) => [args.from.length],
  ```
- **The title must be one of the type's fields.**

  ```ts
  type({ description: 'x', shape: g.obj({ name: g.text() }), display: { label: 'X', title: 'nmae' } });
  ```
- **A stored type's search fields must be its own text fields.**

  ```ts
  type({ description: 'x', shape: g.obj({ name: g.text(), size: g.num() }), display: { label: 'X' }, records: { shape: 'table', owner: 'shared', search: { text: ['size'] } } });
  ```
- **Attachment data is checked.**

  ```ts
  ctx.attach(fs.attachments.diff, { path: args.path }, 'missing before and after');
  ```
- **Grep reports linear progress, not steps.**

  ```ts
  ctx.progress.step('scan', 'active');
  ```
- **May be undefined.**

  ```ts
  // confirm is optional: it may be missing, and the compiler makes us check
  await ctx.handlers.confirm({ command: args.command, reason: 'not in safe list' });
  ```
- **Bash didn't declare this field, so it can't see it.**

  ```ts
  void state.lastQuery;
  ```
- **State values are typed.**

  ```ts
  await ctx.state.set({ cwd: 42 });
  ```
- **Cd declared only cwd, so it can't write another field either.**

  ```ts
  await ctx.state.set({ lastQuery: 'x' });
  ```
- **Cd has no form, so there's nothing to wait for.**

  ```ts
  await ctx.form();
  ```
- **Restart has no resume type, so it can't pause.**

  ```ts
  return ctx.pause({ at: 'now' });
  ```
- **Actions the contract doesn't declare are refused.**

  ```ts
  // fs declares no actions, so it can't implement one
  actions: { rerun: async () => ({ exitCode: 0, stdout: '', stderr: '' }) },
  ```
- **Every native fn must be implemented (most are missing here).**

  ```ts
  void serve(fs, { fns: { read: { async run(_a, ctx) { return ctx.done({ text: '', lines: 0, truncated: false }); } } } }, { hub: '', state: '' });
  ```
- **Save declares an undo type, so it must be undoable.**

  ```ts
  void serve(every, { ...complete, fns: { save: { async run(_a, ctx) { return ctx.done(true, { undo: { id: 'x' } }); } } } }, { hub: '', state: '' });
  ```
- **Every method of a type must be implemented.**

  ```ts
  void serve(every, omit(complete, 'types'), { hub: '', state: '' });
  ```
- **Every kind must be implemented.**

  ```ts
  void serve(every, omit(complete, 'kinds'), { hub: '', state: '' });
  ```
- **Every dynamic context must be implemented.**

  ```ts
  void serve(every, omit(complete, 'context'), { hub: '', state: '' });
  ```
- **Every hook must be implemented.**

  ```ts
  void serve(every, omit(complete, 'hooks'), { hub: '', state: '' });
  ```
- **Every mention provider must be implemented.**

  ```ts
  void serve(every, omit(complete, 'mentions'), { hub: '', state: '' });
  ```
- **Every action must be implemented.**

  ```ts
  void serve(every, omit(complete, 'actions'), { hub: '', state: '' });
  ```
- **The result is checked.**

  ```ts
  void serve(tiny, { fns: { ping: { async run(_a, ctx) { return ctx.done('pang'); } } } }, { hub: '', state: '' });
  ```
- **Tiny declares no hooks, so none can be implemented.**

  ```ts
  void serve(tiny, { fns: { ping: { async run(_a, ctx) { return ctx.done('pong'); } } }, hooks: { extra: async () => [] } }, { hub: '', state: '' });
  ```
- **A fn that isn't in the contract can't be implemented.**

  ```ts
  void serve(tiny, { fns: { ping: { async run(_a, ctx) { return ctx.done('pong'); } }, pong: { async run(_a: {}, ctx: never) { return ctx; } } } }, { hub: '', state: '' });
  ```
- **Only declared steps.**

  ```ts
  ctx.progress.step('verify', 'active');
  ```
- **Args are checked.**

  ```ts
  void ctx.fns.read({ file: args.from });
  ```
- **Only deps can be called.**

  ```ts
  void ctx.fns.remove({ path: args.to });
  ```
- **Copy has a required handler, so it must be passed.**

  ```ts
  void ctx.fns.copy({ from: args.path, to: `${args.path}.bak` });
  ```
- **A method call needs the value it is called on.**

  ```ts
  void ctx.fns.reveal({});
  ```
- **The target is the declared key: a table it knows ('invoices' isn't one) and an id.**

  ```ts
  async search(query) { return [{ label: query, target: { table: 'invoices', id: '7' } }]; },
  ```
- **Only the declared fields exist.**

  ```ts
  void target.name;
  ```
- **Fn args are checked.**

  ```ts
  void view.system.run.bash({ cmd: 'ls' });
  ```
- **Only fns the contract declares can be run.**

  ```ts
  void view.system.run.deploy({});
  ```
- **Only actions the contract declares can be sent, and fs declares none.**

  ```ts
  view.system.send.rerun({ command: 'ls' });
  ```
- **The form value is checked.**

  ```ts
  void ((view: FnViewOf<typeof deploy, 'approve'>) => view.submit({ approved: 'yes' }));
  ```
- **A renderer's props must match what it renders.**

  ```ts
  client(fs, { attachments: { diff: WrongView } });
  ```
- **A functional renderer's props must match too.**

  ```ts
  client(fs, { fns: { read: (view: FnViewOf<typeof fs, 'bash'>) => h('pre', view.args.command) } });
  ```
- **Renderers only for things the contract has.**

  ```ts
  client(fs, { fns: { nope: BashView } });
  ```
- **A runtime native fn's result is checked as well.**

  ```ts
  void runningFs.add('bad', fn({ description: 'Bad.', args: g.obj({}), returns: g.num() }), { async run(_a, ctx) { return ctx.done('x'); } });
  ```
- **A target without `*` is a string, not a pattern: name one agent by its ref.**

  ```ts
  const exact: AgentPattern = 'crm/reviewer';
  ```
- **A target without `*` as a string doesn't compile.**

  ```ts
  context({ description: 'Wrong.', content: 'x', inject: { into: ['crm/reviewer'] } });
  ```
- **A plain fn's ref is not an agent, so it can't be a target.**

  ```ts
  context({ description: 'Wrong.', content: 'x', inject: { into: [crm.fns.lookup] } });
  ```
- **A context ref is not an agent either.**

  ```ts
  context({ description: 'Wrong.', content: 'x', inject: { into: [crm.context.deals] } });
  ```
- **At least one target.**

  ```ts
  context({ description: 'Wrong.', content: 'x', inject: { into: [] } });
  ```
- **A mode is tools or static.**

  ```ts
  context({ description: 'Wrong.', content: 'x', inject: { into: ['*'], mode: 'graph' } });
  ```
- **Requires takes fn refs, not names.**

  ```ts
  context({ description: 'Wrong.', content: 'x', inject: { into: ['*'], requires: ['crm/lookup'] } });
  ```
- **A plain fn's ref is not an agent for startingWith.**

  ```ts
  agents.startingWith(crm.fns.lookup);
  ```
- **Agents.of takes a system, not a fn.**

  ```ts
  agents.of(crm.fns.reviewer);
  ```
- **A detail is full or summary.**

  ```ts
  // an agent's injected section: a detail and a cap, or none
  agent({ description: 'x', args: g.obj({}), returns: g.text(), instructions: 'x', injected: { detail: 'brief' } });
  ```
- **Explains is text.**

  ```ts
  // a hook's explains is text
  hook({ description: 'x', explains: 42 });
  ```
- **`paht` is not one of the args.**

  ```ts
  active: { one: 'Reading {args.paht}…', many: 'Reading {n} files…' },
  ```
- **The result exists only once the run is done.**

  ```ts
  active: { one: 'Reading {output.text}…', many: 'Reading {n} files…' },
  ```
- **A group's runs had different args, so `many` names only {fn} and {n}.**

  ```ts
  active: { one: 'Reading…', many: 'Reading {args.path}…' },
  ```

### Mistakes that compile but are wrong

- **Leaving a hub call out of `grants`.** Each `ctx` call past the base needs its capability in `grants.hub`
  (`ctx.state` needs `state`, `ctx.setting` needs `settings`, `ctx.llm` needs `ai`), and each other system's fn it calls
  needs `fns` and a place in `calls`. Under a hub that enforces them the call throws `NOT_GRANTED` and the fn fails;
  declare them in `system()`, as **The contract** shows.
- **Writing a TypeScript type by hand for a shape a gin type describes.** Infer it: `Infer<typeof X>`, `ValueOf<typeof T>`,
  `ConfigOf<(typeof c)['defs']>`.
- **Referring to things by string.** Use refs (`notes.fns.find`, `hub.fns.ask_user`) in deps, tools, context, links and
  hooks; a string can't be checked.
- **Throwing for an expected case.** Return `ctx.invalid(...)` for a mistake in the args and `ctx.fail('caller', ...,
  { code })` for a refusal; a throw is reported as a failure with the error's text.
- **Using `ctx.fail` for the caller's mistake.** An id that doesn't exist, an empty search or a range the wrong way
  round is `ctx.invalid`: the agent retries it, and the chat shows it softly.
- **Catching `Suspended`.** A general `catch` around a call must rethrow what it doesn't handle; `Suspended` means the run
  is pausing.
- **Ignoring `ctx.signal`.** Pass it to fetch, child processes and long loops, or cancel and timeouts can't stop the run.
- **Undo payloads that point at things that change.** Store what is needed to put things back (the old content as a
  `ContentRef`, the row as it was), and record it with `ctx.undoWith` as soon as the first irreversible step is done.
- **Unstable call keys in pausable fns.** Give `{ key }` to calls in fns that pause, so a resumed run gets results back.
- **Reading `process.env` in feature code.** Put tunables in the env slice and pass them in.
- **Keeping data in the system.** Notes, tasks, contacts, anything kept for people or looked up later, are records of a
  stored type (`type({ records })`, `ctx.data`), so the hub checks, versions, shares and searches them. A JSON file or
  a SQLite database in `ctx.dataDir` is invisible to the hub: no search, no Data page, no widgets, no access rules.
  `ctx.dataDir` is for the system's own keys, caches and working copies.
- **Loading everything to filter it.** `ctx.data.find` with `filters`, `sort`, `limit` and `search` runs in the hub's
  database; a loop over every record (or `limit: 200` and a filter in code) doesn't scale and misses pages. Walk many
  records a page at a time with `cursor`.
- **Keeping state in module variables.** Use session state (`state:`) for values fns share in a chat, stored types for
  data, and the hub's config for config. A process restarts; a module variable doesn't survive it.
- **Writing into the system's own folder at runtime.** Use `ctx.dataDir` and `ctx.cacheDir`.
- **Calling a model provider directly.** Every model call goes through `ctx.llm` (or `harnessAiOf(ctx.llm)`), so the hub
  can choose, budget and record it.
- **Node imports in `shared/`.** The contract is imported by the browser and other systems.
- **A system name that differs from `system.json`.** The build and `serve()` refuse it.
- **`async` with no `await`.** This repository's lint (`require-await`) refuses it: write `run(args, ctx) { return
  Promise.resolve(ctx.done(result)); }` for an implementation that has nothing to wait for.
- **Progress on every item of a long loop.** Report a few dozen times per run.
- **Vague descriptions.** The LLM picks fns by their description and fills args from their types and docs: say exactly
  what the fn does and give examples for args that are easy to get wrong.

## 30. Worked examples

### The notes system, whole

The worked example of this guide, `scripts/docs/example`: every file, verbatim. It is compiled by `npm run typecheck`
and its tests run with `npm test`, so it is a correct, complete system for this version of the SDK. Copy its patterns.

#### `scripts/docs/example/client/FindView.vue`

```vue
<script setup lang="ts">
/**
 * The `find` card: the search, a progress bar while it runs, then the notes found, each with a pin button that sends
 * the `pin` action to the system (it waits in the hub while the system is offline).
 */
import type { FnViewOf } from '@harness/sdk';
import { computed } from 'vue';
import type { notes } from '../shared/index.ts';

// args, result, progress and state are typed by the contract's `find`
const view = defineProps<FnViewOf<typeof notes, 'find'>>();

const searched = computed(() => view.args.query ?? view.state.lastQuery ?? `#${view.args.tag ?? ''}`);
const percent = computed(() => Math.round((view.progress?.fraction ?? 0) * 100));

function pin(id: string, pinned: boolean): void {
  void view.system.send.pin({ id, pinned }).result;
}
</script>

<template>
  <article class="find" :aria-busy="view.status === 'running'">
    <header class="head">Notes matching <strong>{{ searched }}</strong></header>
    <div v-if="view.status === 'running'" class="bar" role="progressbar" :aria-valuenow="percent" aria-valuemin="0" aria-valuemax="100">
      <div class="fill" :style="{ width: `${percent}%` }" />
    </div>
    <ul v-if="view.result" class="list">
      <li v-for="note in view.result.notes" :key="note.id" class="note">
        <span class="title">{{ note.title }}</span>
        <span class="tags">{{ note.tags.join(', ') }}</span>
        <button type="button" class="pin" :disabled="!view.system.online" :aria-pressed="note.pinned" @click="pin(note.id, !note.pinned)">
          {{ note.pinned ? 'Unpin' : 'Pin' }}
        </button>
      </li>
    </ul>
    <p v-if="view.result" class="total">{{ view.result.notes.length }} found{{ view.result.more ? ', more past these' : '' }}</p>
    <p v-if="view.error" class="problem">{{ view.error.message }}</p>
  </article>
</template>

<style scoped>
.find { display: grid; gap: var(--space-2); min-width: 0; }
.head { color: var(--text-dim); font-size: var(--text-sm); }
.bar { height: 4px; border-radius: 2px; background: var(--surface-2); overflow: hidden; }
.fill { height: 100%; background: var(--accent); transition: width var(--fast) var(--ease); }
.list { display: grid; gap: var(--space-1); margin: 0; padding: 0; list-style: none; }
.note { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: var(--space-2); min-width: 0; }
.title { flex: 1; min-width: 0; overflow-wrap: anywhere; }
.tags { color: var(--text-dim); font-size: var(--text-xs); }
.pin { min-height: 28px; border: 1px solid var(--border); border-radius: var(--radius-sm); background: var(--surface); color: var(--text); cursor: pointer; }
.total { margin: 0; color: var(--text-dim); font-size: var(--text-xs); }
.problem { margin: 0; color: var(--danger); }
</style>
```

#### `scripts/docs/example/client/NoteInput.vue`

```vue
<script setup lang="ts">
/**
 * The editor and viewer of a `notes/Note`, used wherever the chat draws one: `!` forms, handler prompts and config forms
 * edit it (`v-model`: it emits a whole new note on every change, and shows the type's problems), and run cards and
 * mention cards show it read-only (`readonly`).
 */
import type { InputViewOf } from '@harness/sdk';
import { computed } from 'vue';
import type { NoteValue, notes } from '../shared/index.ts';

const props = defineProps<InputViewOf<typeof notes, 'Note'>>();
const emit = defineEmits<{ 'update:modelValue': [value: NoteValue] }>();

const current = computed<NoteValue>(() => props.modelValue ?? { title: '', text: '', tags: [], pinned: false });

function change(patch: Partial<NoteValue>): void {
  emit('update:modelValue', { ...current.value, ...patch });
}
function onTitle(event: Event): void {
  if (event.target instanceof HTMLInputElement) { change({ title: event.target.value }); }
}
function onText(event: Event): void {
  if (event.target instanceof HTMLTextAreaElement) { change({ text: event.target.value }); }
}
function onTags(event: Event): void {
  if (event.target instanceof HTMLInputElement) { change({ tags: event.target.value.split(',').map((tag) => tag.trim()).filter((tag) => tag !== '') }); }
}
</script>

<template>
  <article v-if="props.readonly" class="note view">
    <strong class="title">{{ current.title }}</strong>
    <p class="text">{{ current.text }}</p>
    <p class="tags">{{ current.tags.map((tag) => `#${tag}`).join(' ') }}</p>
  </article>
  <div v-else class="note" :class="{ invalid: props.problems.length > 0 }">
    <label class="field">Title<input type="text" :value="current.title" @input="onTitle"></label>
    <label class="field">Text<textarea rows="4" :value="current.text" @input="onText" /></label>
    <label class="field">Tags<input type="text" :value="current.tags.join(', ')" placeholder="work, ideas" @input="onTags"></label>
    <p v-for="problem in props.problems" :key="problem" class="problem">{{ problem }}</p>
  </div>
</template>

<style scoped>
.note { display: grid; gap: var(--space-2); min-width: 0; }
.field { display: grid; gap: var(--space-1); font-size: var(--text-sm); }
.invalid .field input, .invalid .field textarea { border-color: var(--danger); }
.problem { margin: 0; color: var(--danger); font-size: var(--text-xs); }
.view { gap: var(--space-1); }
.text { margin: 0; white-space: pre-wrap; overflow-wrap: anywhere; }
.tags { margin: 0; color: var(--text-dim); font-size: var(--text-xs); }
</style>
```

#### `scripts/docs/example/client/NotesPage.vue`

```vue
<script setup lang="ts">
/**
 * The Notes page (`/s/notes/notes`): every note, searched by the hub (never filtered here), with the search in the URL
 * (`?q=`) so a refresh or a shared link shows the same list. It draws with the app's own frame, search input, load state and markdown,
 * from `host.components`, so it looks like the rest of the app.
 */
import type { PageViewOf } from '@harness/sdk';
import { ref, watch } from 'vue';
import type { NotesContract } from '../shared/index.ts';

const view = defineProps<PageViewOf<NotesContract>>();
const { Frame, Input, LoadState, Markdown } = view.host.components;
// `?q=` bound two ways: typing changes the URL, and the URL fills the box
const search = view.host.query.bind('q');
const records = view.host.hub.caller('records', { lastOnly: true });

const notes = ref<readonly { id: string; title: string; text: string }[]>([]);
const loading = ref(true);
const problem = ref('');

async function load(): Promise<void> {
  loading.value = true;
  try {
    // the hub searches its database: words in the title and text, and by meaning
    const words = search.value.trim();
    const page = await records.call({ type: 'notes/Note', ...(words === '' ? {} : { search: words }) });
    notes.value = page.items.map((record) => {
      const value = record.value;
      const text = typeof value === 'object' && value !== null && !Array.isArray(value) && typeof value['text'] === 'string' ? value['text'] : '';
      return { id: record.id, title: record.title, text };
    });
    problem.value = '';
  } catch (error) {
    problem.value = error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'The notes could not be read.';
  } finally {
    loading.value = false;
  }
}
watch(() => search.value, () => { void load(); }, { immediate: true });
</script>

<template>
  <Frame title="Notes" description="Every note, searched by the hub.">
    <Input v-model="search.value" class="search" type="search" icon="search" label="Search notes" hide-label clearable placeholder="Search notes" />
    <LoadState :loading="loading" :problem="problem" :ready="notes.length > 0" :empty="!loading && notes.length === 0" empty-title="No notes" @retry="load">
      <ul class="notes">
        <li v-for="note in notes" :key="note.id" class="note">
          <h2>{{ note.title }}</h2>
          <Markdown :source="note.text" />
        </li>
      </ul>
    </LoadState>
  </Frame>
</template>

<style scoped>
.search { max-width: 32rem; margin-bottom: var(--space-3); }
.notes { list-style: none; margin: 0; padding: 0; display: grid; gap: var(--space-3); grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fill, minmax(min(100%, 18rem), 1fr)); }
.note { min-width: 0; padding: var(--space-3); border: 1px solid var(--border); border-radius: var(--radius); background: var(--surface); }
.note h2 { margin: 0 0 var(--space-2); font-size: var(--text-md, 1rem); }
</style>
```

#### `scripts/docs/example/client/ReviewForm.vue`

```vue
<script setup lang="ts">
/**
 * The `review` form: the run waits in `await ctx.form()` until the person approves or refuses here; `view.submit` sends
 * the answer, checked against the contract's form type, and the agent gets it as the fn's result.
 */
import type { FnViewOf } from '@harness/sdk';
import { ref } from 'vue';
import type { notes } from '../shared/index.ts';

const view = defineProps<FnViewOf<typeof notes, 'review'>>();
const comment = ref('');

function answer(approved: boolean): void {
  const note = comment.value.trim();
  void view.submit({ approved, ...(note === '' ? {} : { note }) }).result;
}
</script>

<template>
  <form v-if="view.status === 'running'" class="review" @submit.prevent="answer(true)">
    <label class="field">Comment (optional)<input v-model="comment" type="text" autocomplete="off"></label>
    <div class="actions">
      <button type="button" @click="answer(false)">Refuse</button>
      <button type="submit" class="primary">Approve</button>
    </div>
  </form>
  <p v-else-if="view.result" class="answered">{{ view.result.approved ? 'Approved' : 'Refused' }}{{ view.result.note ? `: ${view.result.note}` : '' }}</p>
</template>

<style scoped>
.review { display: grid; gap: var(--space-2); }
.field { display: grid; gap: var(--space-1); font-size: var(--text-sm); }
.actions { display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap; justify-content: flex-end; gap: var(--space-2); }
.primary { background: var(--accent); color: var(--on-accent); }
.answered { margin: 0; color: var(--text-dim); }
</style>
```

#### `scripts/docs/example/client/WordCount.vue`

```vue
<script setup lang="ts">
/**
 * A resource view: a "Words" tab beside the hub's own preview of every markdown resource (notes are served as markdown),
 * counting its words and headings. It reads the text through the view, so it works for any system's markdown.
 */
import type { ResourceView, WithSystem } from '@harness/sdk';
import { ref, watchEffect } from 'vue';
import type { NotesContract } from '../shared/index.ts';

const view = defineProps<ResourceView & WithSystem<NotesContract['defs']>>();
const counts = ref<{ words: number; headings: number } | undefined>(undefined);
const problem = ref('');

watchEffect(() => {
  const version = view.stat.version;
  view.text().then((text) => {
    if (view.stat.version !== version) { return; }
    counts.value = { words: text.split(/\s+/).filter((word) => word !== '').length, headings: text.split('\n').filter((line) => line.startsWith('#')).length };
    problem.value = '';
  }).catch((error: unknown) => { problem.value = error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'It could not be read.'; });
});
</script>

<template>
  <section class="words" aria-live="polite">
    <p v-if="problem" class="problem">{{ problem }}</p>
    <p v-else-if="counts"><strong>{{ counts.words }}</strong> words, <strong>{{ counts.headings }}</strong> headings in {{ view.stat.name }}</p>
    <p v-else class="dim">Counting…</p>
  </section>
</template>

<style scoped>
.words { padding: var(--space-3); }
.words p { margin: 0; }
.dim { color: var(--text-dim); }
.problem { color: var(--danger); }
</style>
```

#### `scripts/docs/example/client/index.ts`

```ts
/**
 * The notes system's renderers, typed by its contract: the `find` card, the `review` form, the note attachment, the
 * editor and viewer of `notes/Note`, a resource view for markdown, and the Notes page. The chat loads this module from
 * the hub and provides each renderer's `system`.
 */
// typescript-eslint can't see the types of .vue modules from .ts files; vue-tsc checks this file
/* eslint-disable @typescript-eslint/no-unsafe-assignment */
// #region client
import { client } from '@harness/sdk';
import { notes } from '../shared/index.ts';
import FindView from './FindView.vue';
import NoteCard from './NoteCard.vue';
import NoteInput from './NoteInput.vue';
import NotesPage from './NotesPage.vue';
import ReviewForm from './ReviewForm.vue';
import WordCount from './WordCount.vue';

export default client(notes, {
  fns: { find: FindView, review: ReviewForm },
  attachments: { note: NoteCard },
  inputs: { Note: NoteInput },
  // a tab beside the hub's preview of every markdown resource; `mode: 'replace'` would draw instead of the preview
  resources: { markdown: { component: WordCount, mode: 'tab', label: 'Words' } },
  // the page the contract's `pages` names, at /s/notes/notes
  pages: { NotesPage },
});
// #endregion client
```

{

  "type": "module",
  "description": "The authoring guide's worked example. Development only: system.json holds the name, version and entries.",
  "exports": {
    ".": "./shared/index.ts",
    "./server": "./server/index.ts",
    "./client": "./client/index.ts"
  },
