#### <a id="core-approvalpreview"></a>`ApprovalPreview`

const, type · declared in `packages/core/src/approvals.ts`

What a run would do, worked out before it is approved.

```ts
type ApprovalPreview = {
  title: string;
  parts: ({
    type: "text";
    text: string;
  } | {
    type: "code";
    language: string;
    code: string;
    title?: string | undefined;
  } | {
    type: "table";
    title: string;
    table: {
      columns: {
        name: string;
        kind: "number" | "boolean" | "date" | "text" | "integer" | "datetime" | "time" | "uuid" | "json" | "binary";
        nullable: boolean;
        label?: string | undefined;
        type?: TypeDef<any> | undefined;
        ref?: `${string}/${string}` | undefined;
        role?: "group" | "measure" | "attribute" | undefined;
        key?: boolean | undefined;
        editable?: boolean | undefined;
      }[];
      rows: z.core.util.JSONType[][];
      offset: number;
      truncated: boolean;
      sort: {
        column: string;
        dir: "asc" | "desc";
      }[];
      limit?: number | undefined;
      total?: number | undefined;
      write?: {
        affected: number;
      } | undefined;
      records?: {
        type: `${string}/${string}`;
        id: string;
      } | undefined;
      drill?: {
        fn: `${string}/${string}`;
        args: Record<string, z.core.util.JSONType>;
        keys: string[];
      } | undefined;
    };
  } | {
    type: "type-diff";
    name: string;
    changes: string[];
    breaks: {
      fn: string;
      why: string;
    }[];
    before?: TypeDef<any> | undefined;
    after?: TypeDef<any> | undefined;
  } | {
    type: "flags";
    flags: {
      path: string;
      kind: "token" | "key" | "gitignored" | "env" | "credential" | "secret-content" | "runs-code" | "unknown-path";
      rule: string;
      access: "read" | "write";
      action: "ask" | "allow" | "refuse";
      why: string;
      certain: boolean;
    }[];
  })[];
  irreversible: boolean;
  subject?: {
    kind: "system-install";
    system: SystemName;
    instance: InstanceName;
    version: string;
    manifestHash: string;
    bundleHash: string;
    sourceHash: string;
    host: {
      system: SystemName;
      instance: InstanceName;
    };
    source?: string | undefined;
    grants?: {
      hub: ("search" | "embed" | "fns" | "settings" | "state" | "resources" | "approvals" | "connections" | "decide" | "data" | "ai")[];
      models?: {
        roles?: ("ocr" | "chat" | "transcribe" | "dictate" | "speak" | "describe" | "decide")[] | undefined;
        models?: string[] | undefined;
        perRun?: number | undefined;
        perDay?: number | undefined;
      } | undefined;
      secrets?: string[] | undefined;
      calls?: (`${string}/${string}` | SystemName)[] | undefined;
      network?: string[] | undefined;
      files?: {
        read: string[];
        write: string[];
      } | undefined;
    } | undefined;
    replaces?: {
      version: string;
      manifestHash: string;
    } | undefined;
  } | undefined;
  checks?: {
    scan: {
      size: number;
      builtins: string[];
      concerns: {
        kind: "builtin" | "dynamic-import" | "dynamic-require" | "eval" | "new-function";
        line: number;
        detail: string;
        code: string;
      }[];
      urls: string[];
      hosts: string[];
    };
    tests?: {
      system: SystemName;
      version: string;
      ranAt: number;
      duration: number;
      passed: number;
      failed: number;
      skipped: number;
      cases: {
        file: string;
        name: string;
        status: "failed" | "skipped" | "passed";
        duration: number;
        real?: boolean | undefined;
        error?: string | undefined;
      }[];
      more?: number | undefined;
      previews?: {
        component: string;
        state: string;
        viewport: "phone" | "desktop";
        theme: "light" | "dark";
        image: {
          hash: string;
          size: number;
          mime: string;
        };
      }[] | undefined;
    } | undefined;
  } | undefined;
  summary?: {
    line: string;
    counts: {
      section: string;
      added: number;
      changed: number;
      removed: number;
    }[];
    flags: {
      flag: "models" | "capabilities" | "secrets" | "effects" | "calls" | "files" | "hosts" | "delete-fns" | "write-fns" | "injects";
      line: string;
      items: string[];
      lead?: string | undefined;
      tail?: string | undefined;
    }[];
    grants?: "added" | "changed" | "widened" | "narrowed" | undefined;
    config?: "removed" | "added" | "changed" | undefined;
  } | undefined;
};
```

#### <a id="core-contextcrawlresult"></a>`ContextCrawlResult`

const, type · declared in `packages/core/src/context-files.ts`

What `hub/ctx_crawl` did: the items it took (`crawled`, in the order taken), those loaded into the session as
`hub/ctx_load` loads them (none when metadata only) with the fns they brought, the starting names it refused,
the links it skipped, why it stopped, and what it spent: the decide calls (`calls`, each a model call), the questions
asked and the tokens gathered. The model reads only its `cards` (`LoadCards`).

```ts

  loaded: `${string}/${string}`[];
  fns: `${string}/${string}`[];
  fnsRefused: {
    code: "paused" | "system-needs-config" | "system-disconnected" | "system-removed" | "kind-missing" | "kind-system-disconnected" | "dep-unavailable" | "disabled" | "held" | "role-unset" | "no-such-fn" | "not-a-name" | "other-system" | "not-for-model" | "connect-account";
    reason: string;
    fn: string;
    system?: SystemName | undefined;
  }[];
  refused: {
    context: string;
    reason: string;
    closest: `${string}/${string}`[];
  }[];
  cards: string;
  crawled: {
    name: `${string}/${string}`;
    description: string;
    depth: number;
    via: "start" | "import" | "choice";
    tokens: number;
    title?: string | undefined;
    from?: `${string}/${string}` | undefined;
    probability?: number | undefined;
    content?: string | undefined;
  }[];
  skipped: {
    context: string;
    depth: number;
    reason: "failed" | "declined" | "max-tokens" | "max-decisions" | "max-depth" | "unavailable";
    from?: `${string}/${string}` | undefined;
    probability?: number | undefined;
  }[];
  stop: {
    reason: "failed" | "max-tokens" | "max-decisions" | "max-depth" | "complete";
    detail?: string | undefined;
  };
  depth: number;
  decisions: number;
  tokens: number;
  calls: string[];
  metadataOnly: boolean;
};
```

#### <a id="core-contextfileheader"></a>`ContextFileHeader`

const, type · declared in `packages/core/src/context-files.ts`

The YAML header of a context file: `ContextDecl` without its content (the body is the content), with `key` for its
name and the lists optional. A summary is required, so every item has a short form for small models. Every link
(`fns`, `imports`, `choices`, `links`, `parent`, `next`) is a qualified name; `inject.into` holds exact agent names
or patterns (`inject: { into: ['hub/chat*'], order: 40 }`).

```ts

  description: string;
  key: string;
  summary: string;
  imports: `${string}/${string}`[];
  fns: `${string}/${string}`[];
  suggestFns: `${string}/${string}`[];
  suggest: "user" | "llm" | "none" | "both";
  visible: "user" | "llm" | "none" | "both";
  tags: string[];
  title?: string | undefined;
  links?: {
    to: {
      kind: "context";
      name: `${string}/${string}`;
    } | {
      kind: "fn";
      name: `${string}/${string}`;
    } | {
      kind: "type";
      name: `${string}/${string}`;
    } | {
      kind: "resource";
      uri: `${string}://${string}`;
      section?: SectionKey | undefined;
    };
    relation: "next" | "about" | "parent" | "imports" | "brings" | "suggests" | "related";
    weight: number;
  }[] | undefined;
  weight?: number | undefined;
  niche?: "admin" | "developer" | "reference" | undefined;
  next?: `${string}/${string}` | undefined;
  inject?: {
    into: string[];
    order: number;
    requires: `${string}/${string}`[];
    mode?: "tools" | "static" | undefined;
  } | undefined;
  command?: {
    name: string;
    args: {
      name: string;
      hint: string;
      required: boolean;
    }[];
  } | undefined;
  parent?: `${string}/${string}` | undefined;
  choices?: {
    to: `${string}/${string}`;
    when: string;
  }[] | undefined;
};
```

```ts
    } | {

      op: "in" | "notIn";

    } | {
      field: string;
      op: "between";
      from: string | number | boolean;
      to: string | number | boolean;
    } | {
      field: string;
      op: "empty" | "notEmpty";
    })[] | undefined;
    sort?: {

    }[] | undefined;
  } | undefined;

};
```


#### <a id="core-fnsearchpage"></a>`FnSearchPage`

const, type · declared in `packages/core/src/fn-facts.ts`

A page of hits; `next` is the offset of the next page, absent on the last. `filtered`: what the admin's search
decisions dropped from this page (a page can then hold fewer hits than its limit while another follows).
`loaded`: what `hub/fns_search` or `hub/ctx_search` loaded of the kept hits (a decision whose action is `load`).

```ts
type FnSearchPage = {
  items: {
    item: "fn" | "context";
    name: `${string}/${string}`;
    system: SystemName;
    description: string;
    available: boolean;
    kind?: string | undefined;
    mode?: "read" | "write" | "delete" | undefined;
    signature?: string | undefined;
    effects?: string | undefined;
    instance?: InstanceName | undefined;
    rank?: number | undefined;
    similarity?: number | undefined;
    matched?: "words" | "both" | "meaning" | undefined;
    section?: {
      key: SectionKey;
      path: string[];
      title: string;
    } | undefined;
    niche?: "admin" | "developer" | "reference" | undefined;
    blocked?: {
      code: "paused" | "system-needs-config" | "system-disconnected" | "system-removed" | "kind-missing" | "kind-system-disconnected" | "dep-unavailable" | "disabled" | "held" | "role-unset" | "no-such-fn" | "not-a-name" | "other-system" | "not-for-model" | "connect-account";
      reason: string;
      system?: SystemName | undefined;
    } | undefined;
    inPrompt?: "injected" | "loaded" | undefined;
    exact?: true | undefined;
  }[];
  next?: number | undefined;
  filtered?: {
    item: "fn" | "context";
    by: "llm" | "decision";
    dropped: {
      name: `${string}/${string}`;
      note?: string | undefined;
    }[];
    calls?: string[] | undefined;
    failed?: string | undefined;
    action?: "load" | undefined;
  }[] | undefined;
  loaded?: {
    fns: `${string}/${string}`[];
    context: `${string}/${string}`[];
    note: string;
  } | undefined;
  missing?: {
    names: `${string}/${string}`[];
    note: string;
  } | undefined;
};
```

#### <a id="core-fnsearchquery"></a>`FnSearchQuery`

const, type · declared in `packages/core/src/fn-facts.ts`

A search over fns and context, done in the hub's database. Without `text` it lists what the filters let through,
by name. Every filter narrows: `kinds` (the fn kinds, such as `input/form` or `database/query`), `systems`,
`instance` (a fleet instance's own fns), `visibility` (what the asker shares: `mine` they own, `shared` others gave
them), `types` (fns that read or write any of these named types), `modes` (fns of these modes), and `names` (exactly
these fns and context items, to look up their hits by name). `filters` are the context list's own filters
(`LIST_FIELDS.contexts`: origin, tags, off, stale, tokens, updated…), run as SQL over the context table, so only
context matches a query with them. Text matches a context item's own text and each of its heading sections; a hit
found by a section says which (`FnSearchHit.section`).

```ts
type FnSearchQuery = {
  text?: string | undefined;
  match?: "text" | "semantic" | "hybrid" | undefined;
  items?: ("fn" | "context")[] | undefined;
  kinds?: (`${string}/${string}` | "native")[] | undefined;
  systems?: SystemName[] | undefined;
  instance?: InstanceName | undefined;
  visibility?: "shared" | "public" | "mine" | undefined;
  types?: {
    reads?: string[] | undefined;
    writes?: string[] | undefined;
  } | undefined;
  modes?: ("read" | "write" | "delete")[] | undefined;
  effects?: {
    thing: "types" | "records" | "fns" | "context" | "examples" | "kinds" | "resources" | "components" | "approvals" | "secrets" | "connections";
    action?: "create" | "update" | "remove" | undefined;
    kind?: string | undefined;
  }[] | undefined;
  names?: `${string}/${string}`[] | undefined;
  filters?: ({
    field: string;
    op: "is" | "isNot" | "gt" | "gte" | "lt" | "lte" | "contains";
    value: string | number | boolean;
  } | {
    field: string;
    op: "in" | "notIn";
    values: (string | number | boolean)[];
  } | {
    field: string;
    op: "between";
    from: string | number | boolean;
    to: string | number | boolean;
  } | {
    field: string;
    op: "empty" | "notEmpty";
  })[] | undefined;
  off?: boolean | undefined;
  scopes?: ("user" | "session" | "everyone")[] | undefined;
  available?: boolean | undefined;
  niches?: ("admin" | "developer" | "reference")[] | undefined;
  order?: "name" | "rank" | undefined;
  offset?: number | undefined;
  limit?: number | undefined;
};
```

```ts
type GinTypeDef = TypeDef<any>;
```

### From `packages/core/src/grants.ts`

#### <a id="core-configcomponentdecl"></a>`ConfigComponentDecl`

const, type · declared in `packages/core/src/grants.ts`

A system's own config component (a Vue component exported by its client bundle under `component`). `replace` draws
it instead of the gin form; `beside` draws it under the form. It gets the config, its gin type, a validate callback
and the system's client; the hub still checks the value with the gin type before saving.

```ts
type ConfigComponentDecl = {
  component: string;
  mode: "replace" | "beside";
};
```

#### <a id="core-systemgrants"></a>`SystemGrants`

const, type · declared in `packages/core/src/grants.ts`

Everything a system may do through the hub and, when fs supervises it, on its machine. A system that declares no
grants gets the base only. The hub refuses (and logs as a `RefusalRecord`) anything outside them.

```ts
type SystemGrants = {
  hub: ("search" | "embed" | "fns" | "settings" | "state" | "resources" | "approvals" | "connections" | "decide" | "data" | "ai")[];
  models?: {
    roles?: ("ocr" | "chat" | "transcribe" | "dictate" | "speak" | "describe" | "decide")[] | undefined;
    models?: string[] | undefined;
    perRun?: number | undefined;
    perDay?: number | undefined;
  } | undefined;
  secrets?: string[] | undefined;
  calls?: (`${string}/${string}` | SystemName)[] | undefined;
  network?: string[] | undefined;
  files?: {
    read: string[];
    write: string[];
  } | undefined;
};
```

### From `packages/core/src/lifecycle.ts`

#### <a id="core-hostrefusal"></a>`HostRefusal`

const, type · declared in `packages/core/src/lifecycle.ts`

A refusal a host saw on the machine (`reportRefusal` on the link): a host its allowlist proxy refused, or a path
Node's permission model refused, for an instance it hosts. The hub keeps it as that instance's `RefusalRecord`.

```ts
type HostRefusal = {
  instance: {
    system: SystemName;
    instance: InstanceName;
  };
  rule: "files" | "network";
  attempt: string;
  detail: string;
};
```

#### <a id="core-previewanswer"></a>`PreviewAnswer`

const, type · declared in `packages/core/src/lifecycle.ts`

A system's answer to a preview (`preview` on the system link): the preview, or why the run can't go ahead at all
(it then fails as invalid input). An install host sends the contract the card would install (`contract`): the hub
checks it against the subject's hash, works out the card's `summary` from it, and never stores it.

```ts

  preview: {
    title: string;
    parts: ({
      type: "text";

    } | {
      type: "code";
      language: string;
      code: string;

      type: "table";

      table: {

          ref?: `${string}/${string}` | undefined;

        total?: number | undefined;

        } | undefined;

        } | undefined;

      };

        rule: string;
        access: "read" | "write";
        action: "ask" | "allow" | "refuse";
```

#### <a id="core-fneffects"></a>`FnEffects`

const, type · declared in `packages/core/src/manifest.ts`

What a fn says it does to data (`FnDecl.effects`): its mode, the named types it reads and writes, what it creates,
updates or removes in the hub, and whether its own work costs nothing. Absent parts are worked out by the hub (`FnFacts`): the mode from the `read` tag, the types
from its signature, and everything a fn uses (its deps, an agent's tools) is folded in.

```ts

  mode?: "read" | "write" | "delete" | undefined;
  reads?: `${string}/${string}`[] | undefined;
  writes?: `${string}/${string}`[] | undefined;
  free?: boolean | undefined;
  registry?: {

    action: "create" | "update" | "remove";
    kind?: `${string}/${string}` | "native" | undefined;
  }[] | undefined;
  keeps?: "resources"[] | undefined;
};
```

#### <a id="core-fnrouting"></a>`FnRouting`

const, type · declared in `packages/core/src/manifest.ts`

Which instance of a fleet runs a fn's calls. `instance`: always that one (a database's query fns), and the
fn belongs to it the way `TypeDecl.instance` says. `any`: whichever connected instance the hub picks first (the
session's pin, then the default), with no ambiguity refusal; the fn forwards with `on` itself when it must.
Absent: the fleet rules (`FleetRouter`).

```ts
type FnRouting = {
  to: "instance";
  instance: InstanceName;
} | {
  to: "any";
};
```

What the hub did with a manifest or patch.

```ts
type ImportReport = {
  added: `${string}/${string}`[];
  updated: `${string}/${string}`[];
```

#### <a id="core-kinddescription"></a>`KindDescription`

const, type · declared in `packages/core/src/manifest.ts`

Everything a kind works out from one payload, so the author of a fn of that kind writes only the payload. The hub
stores this with the fn and recomputes it whenever the payload, or anything it references, changes.

```ts
type KindDescription = {
  args: TypeDef<any>;
  returns: TypeDef<any>;
  deps: `${string}/${string}`[];
  operations: {
    cancel: boolean;
    pause: boolean;
    undo: boolean;
    background: boolean;
  };
  examples: {
    args: z.core.util.JSONType;
    result?: z.core.util.JSONType | undefined;
    note?: string | undefined;
  }[];
  types?: `${string}/${string}`[] | undefined;
  refs?: string[] | undefined;
  handlers?: Record<string, {
    description: string;
    args: TypeDef<any>;
    returns: TypeDef<any>;
    required: boolean;
  }> | undefined;
  progress?: {
    type: "none";
  } | {
    type: "linear";
  } | {
    type: "steps";
    steps: Record<string, string>;
  } | undefined;
  status?: {
    active: {
      one: string;
      many: string;
    };
    retry: {
      one: string;
      many: string;
    };
    done: {
      one: string;
      many: string;
    };
    failed: {
      one: string;
      many: string;
    };
  } | undefined;
  limits?: {
    timeout?: number | undefined;
    retries?: number | undefined;
    cost?: number | undefined;
  } | undefined;
  display?: "message" | "card" | "hidden" | undefined;
  approval?: {
    preview: boolean;
    remember: boolean;
    approver?: "person" | "admins" | undefined;
  } | undefined;
  effects?: {
    mode?: "read" | "write" | "delete" | undefined;
    reads?: `${string}/${string}`[] | undefined;
    writes?: `${string}/${string}`[] | undefined;
    free?: boolean | undefined;
    registry?: {
      thing: "types" | "records" | "fns" | "context" | "examples" | "kinds" | "resources" | "components" | "approvals" | "secrets" | "connections";
      action: "create" | "update" | "remove";
      kind?: `${string}/${string}` | "native" | undefined;
    }[] | undefined;
    keeps?: "resources"[] | undefined;
  } | undefined;
  readable?: {
    language: string;
    code: string;
  } | undefined;
  connectKey?: ConnectKey | undefined;
  parallelClaims?: string[] | undefined;
};
```

#### <a id="core-kindfnshape"></a>`KindFnShape`

const, type · declared in `packages/core/src/manifest.ts`

A fn as a kind's check sees it (sdk `KindCheckCtx.fn`, hub link `fnShape`): its args, result, description and mode
(worked out through what it calls, when the hub has its facts), so a kind can declare the effects of what it calls.

```ts
type KindFnShape = {
  args: TypeDef<any>;
  returns: TypeDef<any>;
  description: string;
  mode: "read" | "write" | "delete";
};
```

#### <a id="core-contributiondecl"></a>`ContributionDecl`

const, type · declared in `packages/core/src/registries.ts`

One entry a system adds to a registry (`Manifest.contributes`). Its component and match logic are in the system's
client bundle, under the same registry and name; this declaration tells the hub and the web app which bundles to load.

```ts
type ContributionDecl = {
  registry: `${string}/${string}`;
  name: string;
  label: string;
  description?: string | undefined;
  icon?: string | undefined;
  shape?: {
    channels: {
      name: string;
      label: string;
      accepts: ("number" | "boolean" | "date" | "text" | "integer" | "datetime" | "time" | "uuid" | "json" | "binary")[];
      required: boolean;
      many: boolean;
      min?: number | undefined;
      role?: "group" | "measure" | undefined;
    }[];
    rows: {
      min: number;
      max?: number | undefined;
    };
    whole: boolean;
    rules: ({
      rule: "distinct";
      channel: string;
      max: number;
    } | {
      rule: "non-negative";
      channel: string;
    } | {
      rule: "bounded";
      channel: string;
    } | {
      rule: "distinct-columns";
      channels: string[];
    } | {
      rule: "links";
      from: string;
      to: string;
    })[];
    styles: {
      name: string;
      label: string;
      whole?: true | undefined;
      needs?: string[] | undefined;
    }[];
    claims: {
      claim: "range";
      channel: string;
    }[];
    priority: {
      band: "floor" | "structural" | "encoded" | "time-encoded";
      terms: ("temporal-key" | "low-cardinality" | "enum-key" | "high-null" | "many-categories")[];
    };
    size?: "window" | "top-n" | "partition" | "aggregate" | undefined;
    pageSize?: number | undefined;
  } | undefined;
};
```

#### <a id="core-invalidinput"></a>`InvalidInput`

const, type · declared in `packages/core/src/run.ts`

A run's input was refused before (or by) its fn. This is retryable and often expected from a model: the hub feeds the
problems back to the agent and the chat shows the run calmly as "Thinking harder" (the model's slip, with no bearing on

```ts
  source: "schema" | "result" | "json" | "unavailable" | "validation";

    path: string;
    message: string;
  }[];
  raw?: string | undefined;
  blocked?: {
    code: "paused" | "system-needs-config" | "system-disconnected" | "system-removed" | "kind-missing" | "kind-system-disconnected" | "dep-unavailable" | "disabled" | "held" | "role-unset" | "no-such-fn" | "not-a-name" | "other-system" | "not-for-model" | "connect-account";
    reason: string;
    system?: SystemName | undefined;
  } | undefined;
```
```ts
};
```

### From `packages/core/src/system-providers.ts`

#### <a id="core-providermodeldecl"></a>`ProviderModelDecl`

const, type · declared in `packages/core/src/system-providers.ts`

One model a system's provider serves. `capabilities` are aeye `ModelCapability` names (`chat`, `tools`, `vision`,
`json`, `embedding`, `imageEmbedding`, `crossModalEmbedding`, `decision`, ...); the hub keeps only those aeye knows.
`caching`: whether the model reads a prompt cache. An embedding model (of text or images) gives its vectors'
`dimensions` and the most characters it reads (`maxInput`); a
decision model the most questions it answers in one call (`maxQuestions`) and the most bytes of input and questions
(`maxRequestBytes`), which every caller splits by.

```ts
type ProviderModelDecl = {
  id: string;
  capabilities: string[];
  contextWindow: number;
  pricing: {
    input: number;
    output: number;
    cached?: number | undefined;
    cacheWrite?: number | undefined;
  };
  caching: boolean;
  name?: string | undefined;
  maxOutput?: number | undefined;
  dimensions?: number | undefined;
  maxInput?: number | undefined;
  maxQuestions?: number | undefined;
  maxRequestBytes?: number | undefined;
};
```

#### <a id="core-argplan"></a>`ArgPlan`

const, type · declared in `packages/core/src/widgets.ts`

How a widget shows one argument of its source: a chip in the control row (labelled, limited to choices, with a
default), `pinned` to a value (it leaves the widget's args; no chip), or `hidden` (no chip; it takes its default, so
only an argument with a default may be hidden). An argument with no plan is a chip with the source's own words.

```ts
type ArgPlan = {
  show: "control";
  label?: string | undefined;
  choices?: {
    label: string;
    value?: {
      literal: z.core.util.JSONType;
    } | {
      relative: "now" | "today" | "today-1d" | "today+1d" | "today-7d" | "today-30d" | "today+7d" | "startOf(week)" | "endOf(week)" | "startOf(month)" | "endOf(month)" | "startOf(month,today-1mo)" | "startOf(quarter)" | "startOf(year)" | "now-1h" | "now-24h";
    } | undefined;
  }[] | undefined;
  custom?: boolean | undefined;
  default?: {
    literal: z.core.util.JSONType;
  } | {
    relative: "now" | "today" | "today-1d" | "today+1d" | "today-7d" | "today-30d" | "today+7d" | "startOf(week)" | "endOf(week)" | "startOf(month)" | "endOf(month)" | "startOf(month,today-1mo)" | "startOf(quarter)" | "startOf(year)" | "now-1h" | "now-24h";
  } | undefined;
} | {
  show: "pinned";
  value: {
    literal: z.core.util.JSONType;
  } | {
    relative: "now" | "today" | "today-1d" | "today+1d" | "today-7d" | "today-30d" | "today+7d" | "startOf(week)" | "endOf(week)" | "startOf(month)" | "endOf(month)" | "startOf(month,today-1mo)" | "startOf(quarter)" | "startOf(year)" | "now-1h" | "now-24h";

} | {

};
```

const, type · declared in `packages/core/src/widgets.ts`

The actions offered on rows of these types (hub `rowActions` and `hub/row_actions`), narrowed by a widget's list. With
no row types (a source with no records type or ref columns) only the fns the widget binds are offered.

```ts
  types: `${string}/${string}`[];

    fn: `${string}/${string}`;
    bind: Record<string, string>;
    label?: string | undefined;
    confirm?: true | undefined;
    then?: "status" | "open" | "refresh" | undefined;
  }[] | undefined;
```
```ts
};
```

#### <a id="core-widgetdrill"></a>`WidgetDrill`

const, type · declared in `packages/core/src/widgets.ts`

What clicking a bar, slice, point or row runs: a read fn returning a table, its args from the clicked row's columns,
opened as a new level with a breadcrumb. Without one, a click opens the row's action menu.

```ts
  fn: `${string}/${string}`;
  label: string;
  bind: Record<string, string>;
};
```

#### <a id="core-widgetpager"></a>`WidgetPager`

const, type · declared in `packages/core/src/widgets.ts`

How the buffered pager asks for more: the paging args, the window the run applied (`limit`), and whether more may
follow (a full window means there may be; a short one is the last). The pager pages locally through what it holds and
runs the widget again (offset = rows held) only when a page needs rows it lacks.

```ts
type WidgetPager = {
  offset: string;
  limit: string;
  window: number;
  more: boolean;
};
```
